</details>


### [111] [Learning Agile Navigation in Crowded Environments for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15036)
*Shuyu Wu,Zeyu Liu,Tianbao Zhang,Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Mingkang Xiong,Wei Xi,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VOP-Nav是一种结合速度障碍几何安全性与端到端学习灵活性的四足机器人导航系统，通过VOP-Net处理多帧激光雷达数据隐式编码动态约束并预测安全速度区域，在仿真和真实环境中均实现了更高的导航成功率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在折衷：基于速度障碍的模型方法理论上保证安全但依赖精确障碍物运动估计（在密集人群中易失败），而端到端学习方法鲁棒但缺乏障碍物运动预测能力，导致碰撞或保守行为。亟需结合两者优势的导航方案。

Method: 提出VOP-Nav系统，核心为VOP-Net网络：利用多帧激光雷达点云隐式编码动态环境约束，基于速度障碍理论预测安全速度区域；VO预测在推理时作为导航策略输入，在训练时作为奖励信号引导安全运动；完全基于局部机载观测，避免显式障碍物检测与跟踪。

Result: 在Isaac Gym仿真中，VOP-Nav在平衡运动速度与避碰能力的同时，成功率超越所有基线方法；在Unitree Go2四足机器人上的真实部署验证了其在复杂室内外动态环境中的鲁棒性与效率。

Conclusion: VOP-Nav成功融合了基于模型方法的几何安全保障与学习方法的适应性，为解决四足机器人在动态密集环境中的导航难题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Navigating dynamic and crowded environments presents significant challenges for quadruped robots due to severe sensor occlusion and unpredictable human motion. Existing approaches face a trade-off: model-based methods, such as Velocity Obstacles (VO), theoretically guarantee safety but rely on accurate obstacle motion estimates that often fail in dense crowds, while end-to-end learning methods offer robustness but lack motion prediction capability of obstacles, leading to collisions or conservative behaviors. To solve this, we propose VOP-Nav, a novel navigation system that combines the geometric safety of VO with the agile adaptability of end-to-end learning. Using only local onboard observations, our system avoids explicit obstacle detection and tracking pipelines. The VOP-Net processes multi-frame LiDAR data to implicitly encode dynamic constraints and predict a safe velocity region derived from Velocity Obstacle theory. Importantly, the VO predictions serve a dual role: they are used as input to the navigation policy during inference and as a reward signal during training to encourage safe motion. Evaluations in Isaac Gym demonstrate that VOP-Nav achieves higher success rates than all baselines while balancing locomotion speed and collision avoidance. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates the system's robustness and efficiency in complex indoor and outdoor dynamic environments.

</details>


### [112] [DriftWorld: Fast World Modeling through Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15065)
*Susie Lu,Haonan Chen,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriftWorld提出基于漂移生成模型的动作条件世界模型，通过单次前向传播生成未来帧（30+ FPS，比扩散模型快17倍），在多个机器人操控基准上实现SOTA决策性能，并可作为离线模拟器以0.99相关性评估策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型作为世界模型时，推理时多步采样导致 rollout 生成昂贵，限制了大规模动作搜索，存在速度瓶颈。

Method: 提出 DriftWorld，基于漂移生成模型（drifting generative models），在训练阶段学习动作条件的漂移，推理时从当前观测和候选动作序列通过单次前向传播直接生成未来帧。

Result: 在 Bridge-V2、RT-1、Language Table、Push-T、Robomimic 等基准上实现SOTA决策性能；推理速度平均比扩散基线快17倍（30+ FPS）；作为离线模拟器时，rollout 评分与真实策略性能相关性高达0.99。

Conclusion: 漂移模型非常适合机器人世界建模，能够快速、高质量地生成想象结果，直接支持规划与策略评估。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable robots to plan by imagining the outcomes of their actions, but their value for control hinges on generating many rollouts quickly. This creates a bottleneck for diffusion-based world models: multistep sampling makes each rollout expensive, limiting large-scale action search at inference time. We introduce DriftWorld, an action-conditioned world model based on drifting generative models. Rather than denoising iteratively at inference, DriftWorld learns an action-conditioned drift during training, allowing it to generate future frames from the current observation and a candidate action sequence in a single forward pass at 30+ fps, which is 17x faster on average than diffusion based baselines. We evaluate DriftWorld on standard vision-based robotic manipulation benchmarks, including Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, and Robomimic. By producing rollouts that are both accurate and fast, DriftWorld achieves state-of-the-art decision-making performance with far less inference time than diffusion-based world model baselines. Beyond online control, DriftWorld can also serve as an offline simulator for ranking real-world robot policies, with rollout-based scores correlating with ground truth at up to 0.99. These results show that drifting models are a strong fit for robot world modeling, where fast, high-quality imagination directly supports planning and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [113] [Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Distributed ISAC-Enabled Vehicle Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15111)
*Wenjie Liu,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无信号灯交叉路口的分布式集成感知与通信（ISAC）框架，利用统一的目标导向语义通信（GSC）机制，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波预测车辆状态、掩膜混合近端策略优化（MHPPO）联合决策感知与命令传输，以及不确定性感知传输设计（UTD），在显著降低信令开销的同时实现100%无碰撞车辆协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将感知、通信与控制分离处理，导致冗余传输、状态信息过时以及车辆协调不可靠，亟需一种融合感知与通信的协同方案来提高交叉路口信令效率与协调可靠性。

Method: 1）采用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）预测车辆状态并融合分布式感知测量；2）提出掩膜混合近端策略优化（MHPPO）框架，基于信息价值（VoI）奖励联合决定感知传输决策、命令传输决策及命令内容；3）提出不确定性感知传输设计（UTD），包括鲁棒波束赋形和基于VoI的时分功率分配。

Result: 所提框架实现了100%无碰撞车辆协调，且相较于基于最新研究的预测性ISAC基线和多种消融基线，显著降低了信令开销。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式ISAC与GSC框架能够有效融合感知与通信功能，在保证安全的同时提升信令效率，为无信号灯交叉路口的车辆协调提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections relies on accurate real-time vehicle state acquisition and reliable command-and-control (C&C) signal delivery. However, existing studies typically treat sensing, communication, and control separately, which may lead to redundant transmissions, outdated state information, and unreliable vehicle coordination. In this paper, we investigate a new scenario of distributed integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled vehicle coordination at intersections, where multiple roadside units (RSUs) collaboratively transmit sensing signals for vehicle state acquisition and C&C signals for vehicle movement control under the management of a central base station (BS). To improve signaling efficiency, we propose a unified goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework, which transmits sensing and C&C signals only when they are semantically important for improving intersection traffic throughput. Specifically, an extended Kalman filter (EKF) is adopted to predict vehicle states and fuse distributed sensing measurements. A masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) framework is then developed to jointly determine sensing transmission decisions, C&C transmission decisions, and C&C signal contents based on a value-of-information (VoI) reward. Furthermore, we propose an uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD), including robust beamforming and VoI-based time-division power allocation, to improve sensing and communication reliability under vehicle state uncertainty and inter-RSU interference. Simulation results show that our proposed framework achieves 100% collision-free vehicle coordination with significantly reduced signaling overhead compared with predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art related studies and several ablation baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Catch, Throw, Repeat: Planning for Human-Robot Partner Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15129)
*Jonathan Rainer Lippert,Kai Ploeger,Abir Chowdhury,Hermann Müller,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人机合作抛接球的实时规划与控制架构，能够在多球同步模式下实现机器人稳定接球与投掷，并在用户研究中实现了三球级联抛接，显著超越此前最佳记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机之间的动态物体交换因感知、时序和接触交互中的不确定性而具有挑战性，人机抛接球作为其极端实例，需要精确的实时协调、带反馈的预测运动规划以及对人类运动变异性具有鲁棒性。实现此类技能对推进物理人机交互和共享自主性具有重要意义。

Method: 提出实时规划与控制架构，集成预测性球体追踪、基于多重打靶法的自适应在线轨迹优化，以及基于状态机的协调逻辑，实现人机多球同步抛接。

Result: 在8名不同技能水平参与者的用户研究中，系统实现了人机共享的三球级联抛接。所有参与者在10分钟测试中均超越此前最佳结果，一名参与者将共享三球级联抛接记录提升5倍至连续20次机器人接球，另一名参与者在单球接发场景中实现100%成功率（连续40次接球）。

Conclusion: 所提架构能够有效支持高动态、高不确定性的人机协同抛接任务，在实时性和鲁棒性上均取得显著提升，为物理人机交互中的动态物体交换提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. Video documentation can be found at https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling

</details>


### [115] [Assessing Physical Frailty and Fall-Risk Indicators with Social Robots: An in situ Evaluation with Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15156)
*Aniol Civit,Antonio Andriella,Alba Martínez,Joan Ars,Aida Ribera,Cristian Barrué,Guillem Alenyà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于社交机器人的衰弱评估框架，利用行为树架构和视觉骨架追踪技术，引导老年人完成标准化衰弱与跌倒风险测试（SPPB和TUG），并在康复中心进行了6个月、81名参与者的实地验证。结果显示机器人与治疗师评估在测试完成时间和步态参数上具有高度一致性（ICC > 0.9），SPPB总分一致性为k=0.67（与治疗师）和k=0.55（与IMU），表明社交机器人可在医疗场景中提供可靠、客观的衰弱评估并收集额外运动功能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的衰弱评估依赖人工操作和粗粒度临床指标（如任务完成时间），耗费资源且忽略了生物力学层面的功能衰退迹象，亟需自动化、精细化的评估手段。

Method: 设计了一个基于行为树架构的机器人框架，集成了感知（视觉骨架追踪）、决策、交互与测量模块，引导老年人完成SPPB和TUG测试，并同步采集临床分数与额外衰弱相关指标。框架经医护人员共同设计，在康复中心研究实验室进行了6个月、N=81的实地评估。

Result: 机器人与治疗师评估在测试完成时间和步态参数上展现出优秀的一致性（ICC > 0.9）；SPPB总分一致性：机器人与治疗师k=0.67（高度一致），机器人与IMU k=0.55（中等一致）。

Conclusion: 社交机器人能够为医疗场景提供可靠、客观的衰弱评估，并能收集超越传统临床结局的相关运动功能指标，具有替代或辅助人工评估的潜力。

Abstract: Frailty assessments are crucial to evaluate the risk of adverse events and the health and social care needs of older adults, yet their administration remains resource-intensive and typically relies on coarse clinical outcomes, such as task completion times, which may overlook biomechanical indicators of functional decline. To address this, we present a robotic framework that guides older adults through standardised frailty and fall-risk tests while capturing clinical scores and additional frailty-related metrics, offering a deeper insight into a user's condition. The system uses a Behaviour Tree architecture that coordinates perception, decision-making, interaction, and measurement modules. Using vision-based skeleton tracking, the robot evaluates established clinical tests, including the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) and the Timed Up and Go (TUG). The framework was co-designed with healthcare professionals and evaluated in situ during six months in a rehabilitation centre's research lab with N=81 older adults. Robot-derived measurements were compared against therapist assessments and clinical reference instruments, including a gait analysis walkway and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). Results showed excellent agreement for most test completion times and gait-related parameters ($ICC > 0.9$). And, substantial agreement for the overall SPPB score comparing the robot and the therapist ($k = 0.67$) and moderate agreement comparing the robot and the IMU ($k=0.55$). The findings highlight that social robots can provide reliable and objective frailty assessments in healthcare settings while enabling the collection of relevant mobility indicators beyond conventional outcomes.

</details>


### [116] [Scaling Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15163)
*Weishuai Zeng,Kangning Yin,Xiaojie Niu,Shunlin Lu,Weixiang Zhong,Jiahe Chen,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Zirui Wang,Furui Xu,Ming Zhou,Kailin Li,Weinan Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人形机器人的行为基础模型（BFM）的规模化配方，通过协调运动跟踪学习范式、策略性协同（在线策略数据量与参考动作多样性）以及可扩展的Humanoid Transformer架构，实现了控制精度和任务泛化能力的显著提升，在全局模式下MPKPE降低82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人控制需要全身协调、实时响应和泛化能力，现有BFM虽具潜力但规模化扩展时缺乏对学习范式、数据与架构三者协同关系的清晰认识。

Method: 1) 采用全局坐标系下全身行为复现的运动跟踪学习范式；2) 策略性协同在线策略采样数量与参考动作多样性；3) 提出Humanoid Transformer作为可扩展的表达性模型架构，促使结构化行为表征自然涌现。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，本地模式下MPKPE降低超10%，全局模式下MPKPE降低82%，显著优于现有控制器。

Conclusion: 所提出的规模化配方使得BFM成为可扩展且通用的人形机器人控制的有效基础方法。

Abstract: Humanoid control requires natural whole-body coordination, precise real-time responses to control signals, and robust generalization across diverse environmental contexts, making it a cornerstone for generalist embodied agents. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have recently emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by leveraging large-scale behavioral data to achieve superior expressiveness, versatility and generalization. However, despite growing interest in scaling BFMs to further improve their capabilities, it remains unclear how key factors, including the learning paradigm, behavioral data and model architecture should be coordinated to enable effective scaling. In this work, we revisit the scaling recipe for BFMs and demonstrate that substantial performance gains can be achieved through the coordination of three core components: 1) the learning paradigm of motion tracking that reformulates diverse humanoid control problems as the reproduction of integrated whole-body behaviors in the global frame; 2) the strategic synergy between on-policy rollout quantity and reference motion diversity; and 3) the expressive and scalable model architecture termed Humanoid Transformer that facilitates the natural emergence of structured behavioral representations. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world deployment, we demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in control fidelity and task generalization, reducing Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) on the test set by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared with existing humanoid controllers. These results establish BFM as a principled and effective foundation for scalable and general-purpose humanoid control.

</details>


### [117] [AHEAD: Anticipatory Hand-Driven Teleoperation via Human Intent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15172)
*Seok Joon Kim,Junho Lee,Federica Spinola,Taein Kwon,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AHEAD是一个实时VR遥操作系统，通过注意力分类器预测操作者意图（抓取对象和放置槽位），实现主动式手控操作，相比基线方法，机器人反应延迟降低0.6-1.4秒，同时降低操作者工作负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接手控遥操作虽然精确但需要持续监控和修正，疲劳度高；监督式遥操作虽然简化操作但引入等待延迟。如何减少机器人反应时间同时降低操作者工作负荷是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出AHEAD系统：在数字孪生中，操作者通过手部动作传达高级指令；系统利用注意力分类器处理短窗口的3D手部/头部信号及场景上下文，预测意图抓取对象和放置槽位；结合状态机将意图预测转化为稳定的机器人目标，在噪声预测和修正手部动作下保持稳定。

Result: 意图预测模块Top1准确率：抓取对象76%，目标槽位76%。用户研究表明，相比基线，机器人反应延迟降低0.6秒（对象）和1.4秒（槽位），操作者报告更低的负荷。

Conclusion: AHEAD通过意图预测实现了主动式手控遥操作，兼顾了快速响应和低操作负荷，有效解决了传统方法在精度与效率之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Direct hand-driven teleoperation maps an operator's hand motion to robot end-effector commands at every frame, enabling precise control, but it requires constant monitoring and correction during approach, grasp, and placement, which can be slow and fatiguing. For repetitive pick-and-place tasks, supervisory (goal-based) teleoperation simplifies this process: the operator specifies goals/waypoints, and the robot executes the motion using planning algorithms. Yet, this introduces latency, as the robot must wait for the next command before it can plan and act. "How can we reduce robot reaction time while lowering operator workload?" To tackle this question, we present AHEAD, a real-time VR teleoperation system that anticipates operator intent to enable proactive, hand-driven control. In a digital twin, the operator performs pick-and-place naturally, using hand motion to convey high-level commands rather than a continuous robot trajectory. AHEAD processes a short window of 3D hand and head signals together with scene context through an attention-based classifier to predict the intended grasp object and placement slot. A state machine converts intent predictions into stable robot goals, enabling early motion while remaining stable under noisy predictions and corrective hand movements. AHEAD's intent prediction module achieves Top1 accuracy: 76% for grasp objects and 76% for target slots. Moreover, our user study shows AHEAD reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 s (object) and 1.4 s (slot) relative to baselines. Participants also reported lower operator load, indicating faster robot responses while maintaining low operator effort in practice.

</details>


### [118] [RoboTTT: Context Scaling for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15275)
*Yunfan Jiang,Yevgen Chebotar,Ruijie Zheng,Fengyuan Hu,Yunhao Ge,Jimmy Wu,Tianyuan Dai,Scott Reed,Li Fei-Fei,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboTTT是一种将测试时训练（TTT）集成到机器人基础模型中的方法，通过将视觉运动上下文扩展到8000个时间步（比现有技术高三个数量级）且不增加推理延迟，实现了从人类视频演示的一次性上下文模仿、在线策略改进、对扰动的鲁棒性以及长程多阶段任务上的更强性能。在真实机器人操作任务上，性能相比单步基线提升87%，且训练上下文从1K扩展到8K时间步使性能提升62%，表明上下文长度可作为机器人基础模型的新扩展轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础模型仅能处理单步或短历史视觉运动上下文，缺乏长程上下文建模能力，限制了机器人执行复杂长时序任务、从人类演示中学习以及适应环境变化的能力。

Method: 将测试时训练（TTT）集成到视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中，构建序列模型，其循环状态由快速权重（通过梯度下降在训练和推理期间更新）组成，将历史压缩到权重空间并检索上下文信息。训练时采用序列动作强制与截断式时间反向传播（TBPTT）相结合的方法来扩展上下文长度。

Result: 在真实机器人操作任务上，RoboTTT相比单步上下文基线整体性能提升87%；成功完成一个五分钟、十阶段的组装任务（所有基线均未成功）；8K时间步上下文训练的模型比1K时间步训练的模型性能提升62%。

Conclusion: 首次观察到预训练上下文长度扩展带来闭环性能的稳定提升，验证了上下文长度作为机器人基础模型新扩展轴的有效性，为机器人长程操作、少样本模仿学习和鲁棒策略改进提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/

</details>

### [119] [Spinless charged excitation at the interface between a conventional topological insulator and a topological Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14221)
*Cesar A. Gallegos,Andrew J. Millis,Steven R. White*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过张量网络计算，研究了三角晶格Hofstadter-Hubbard模型中整数量子霍尔相与手性自旋液体相之间的界面，发现界面处存在束缚的自旋中性带电激发模，并分别表征了两体相的体激发性质（手性自旋液体中的分数化激发和整数量子霍尔相中的自旋三重态激子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索拓扑绝缘体相（整数量子霍尔相）与拓扑有序相（手性自旋液体相）之间界面的物理性质，特别是界面处的束缚激发模式及其输运特性。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）对三角晶格Hofstadter-Hubbard模型进行大规模计算，通过空间变化的相互作用强度实现不同区域的不同拓扑相，利用电荷泵浦、自旋泵浦和低能激发态直接计算表征界面模。

Result: 1) 界面处存在自旋中性的带电束缚激发模；2) 手性自旋液体相中观察到分数化激发；3) 整数量子霍尔相中观察到自旋三重态激子形成。

Conclusion: 界面束缚模的存在揭示了不同拓扑相之间界面的丰富物理，为理解拓扑相界面的输运和激发特性提供了新见解。

Abstract: We investigate the interface separating two topologically distinct insulating phases of matter using extensive density-matrix renormalization group calculations to study the triangular-lattice Hofstadter-Hubbard model with a spatially varying interaction strength, chosen to realize both integer quantum Hall and chiral spin liquid states in different spatial regions. We find that the integer quantum Hall-chiral spin liquid interface hosts a spinless charged excitation that is bound to the interface. This mode at the interface is identified through charge and spin pumping, and by direct calculations of low-lying excited states. We also characterize bulk excitations in both phases, finding evidence for fractionalization in the chiral spin liquid and for spin-triplet exciton formation in the integer quantum Hall phase.

</details>


### [120] [AutoHF: a general Hartree-Fock solver utilizing direct energy minimization with automatic differentiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14263)
*Ryan Levy,Brandon Eskridge,Lukas Weber,Miguel A. Morales,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: autohf是一个通用、易用的平均场求解器，通过自动微分和优化技术直接最小化变分能量，自动找到量子多费米子哈密顿量的最优Slater行列式，无需用户为每个哈密顿量手动推导平均场形式并编写定制程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法中，每个量子多体哈密顿量都需要用户手动推导其平均场形式并编写相应的定制程序，过程繁琐且易出错。autohf旨在让用户绕过这一过程，直接通过变分法求解最优Slater行列式。

Method: 采用变分方法，将最优Slater行列式表示为轨道系数的函数，并施加对称性约束；利用机器学习社区发展的自动微分和优化工具，直接最小化变分能量⟨H⟩，从而自动找到最优解。

Result: autohf作为一个通用平均场求解器，能够自动处理多种量子多费米子哈密顿量，无需为每个哈密顿量单独编写程序。

Conclusion: 通过结合变分原理与机器学习中的自动微分和优化工具，autohf提供了一种通用、易用的平均场求解方案，显著降低了量子多体问题平均场求解的门槛。

Abstract: We present autohf, a general, easy-to-use mean-field solver for quantum many-fermion Hamiltonians. It allows the user to bypass the process of deciphering the mean-field form for each many-body Hamiltonian $H$ and thus avoid setting up a tailored program for each $H$. Rather, autohf finds the optimal Slater determinant $|Ψ\rangle$, written in terms of orbital coefficients and subject to symmetry constraints, by directly minimizing the variational energy $\langle H \rangle$. By embracing this variational approach, autohf makes use of the growing power of automatic differentiation and optimization tools developed by the machine learning community.

</details>


### [121] [The two-particle density matrix of a Luttinger liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14402)
*Harini Radhakrishnan,Matthias Thamm,Hatem Barghathi,Bernd Rosenow,Adrian Del Maestro*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用构造性玻色化方法，推导了自旋less费米子在Tomonaga-Luttinger液体中有限尺寸的等时双粒子约化密度矩阵闭合解析表达式，揭示了除已知Luttinger参数依赖的指数γ²外，还存在第二个指数λ控制手征间关联，并在对角极限下复现密度关联与静态结构因子，非对角部分则解析描述电荷密度波和p-wave配对关联，通过与DMRG计算对比验证了其定量准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双粒子相干性是约化密度矩阵层次中首个包含单粒子可观测量无法触及的关联的层级，但即使在低维系统中解析的双粒子密度矩阵也极为罕见，因此需要发展解析方法来获取这一重要信息。

Method: 采用具有显式紫外截断的构造性玻色化方法，推导出自旋less费米子在Tomonaga-Luttinger液体中的有限尺寸等时双粒子约化密度矩阵闭合表达式。

Result: 除已知的Luttinger参数K依赖的指数γ²=(K+K⁻¹-2)/2外，还发现了第二个指数λ=(K⁻¹-K)/2，它编码了相反手征性之间的关联并控制非对角结构。对角极限给出密度关联和静态结构因子，非对角部分解析地描述了排斥作用下的2kF电荷密度波关联和吸引作用下的奇宇称p-wave配对关联。通过单粒子密度矩阵确定截断后，定量重现了Luttinger液体区域中相互作用J-V链的DMRG计算结果。

Conclusion: 该结果将普适的Luttinger液体标度行为与有限微观系统中的可观测量联系起来，为理解低维强关联系统中的双粒子关联提供了精确的解析工具。

Abstract: Two-particle coherence is the first level of the reduced-density-matrix hierarchy that contains correlations inaccessible to single-particle observables, yet analytic two-particle density matrices are rare even in one dimension. We derive a closed, finite-size expression for the equal-time two-particle reduced density matrix of spinless fermions in a Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid using constructive bosonization with an explicit ultraviolet cutoff. In addition to the familiar Luttinger parameter $K$-dependent exponent $γ^2=(K+K^{-1}-2)/2$ which governs the spatial decay of matrix elements, the result exposes a second exponent, $λ=(K^{-1}-K)/2$, which encodes correlations between opposite chiralities and controls the off-diagonal structure. The diagonal limit of the two-particle reduced density matrix yields density correlations and the static structure factor, while its coherences resolve algebraic $2k_F$ charge-density-wave correlations for repulsion and odd-parity p-wave pairing correlations for attraction. After fixing the cutoff from the one-particle density matrix, the analytic result quantitatively reproduces density matrix renormalization group calculations of the interacting J-V chain within the Luttinger liquid regime. The result connects universal Luttinger liquid scaling with observables in finite microscopic systems.

</details>


### [122] [Emergence of a monopole phase in the $J_1{-}J_2$ Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice for small magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14766)
*Sasank Budaraju,Shi Feng,Josef Willsher,Johannes Knolle,Frank Pollmann,Federico Becca*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用变分蒙特卡洛方法，研究三角晶格上J₁-J₂海森堡模型在外加塞曼场下的基态相图，重点探究J₂/J₁=1/8处自旋液相在磁场下的演化，发现了稳定的单极子凝聚相（具有标量手征性但无横向磁序），并通过场论方法确认了其稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究三角晶格J₁-J₂海森堡模型中（零场下）自旋液相在外加磁场下的命运，即自旋液相是否会因磁场而改变，以及会涌现出何种新的量子相。

Method: 采用变分蒙特卡洛方法，在0 ≤ J₂/J₁ ≤ 0.2和0 ≤ H/J₁ ≤ 2的参数空间中，对多个候选态的简单变分波函数在大团簇上进行能量比较，得到相图；并结合包含自洽随机相位近似的场论方法，确认单极子相的稳定性。

Result: 1) J₂/J₁ ≲ 1/6时，存在从无能隙"Y"相到有能隙"up-up-down"相的连续相变；2) J₂/J₁ ≈ 1/8处，在弱磁场区域出现稳定的单极子凝聚相，该相具有有限标量手征性但无横向磁序；3) 无自由参数地确定了单极子相与Y相之间的边界。

Conclusion: 三角晶格J₁-J₂海森堡模型在J₂/J₁ ≈ 1/8处，零场下的自旋液相在弱塞曼场下会演化为稳定的单极子凝聚相，这是一种具有标量手征性但无横向磁序的新奇量子态，其边界可由理论无自由参数确定。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state phase diagram of the $J_1{-}J_2$ Heisenberg model on the triangular lattice under an external Zeeman field $H$ by using the variational Monte Carlo approach. We span a region with $0 \le J_2/J_1 \le 0.2$ and $0 \le H/J_1 \le 2$, to assess the fate of the (putative) spin-liquid phase that has been detected for $J_2/J_1=1/8$ at zero magnetic field. Simple variational ansatze are proposed for a few candidate states, and their energetics are compared on large clusters to obtain the phase diagram. For $J_2/J_1 \lesssim 1/6$, a continuous transition from a gapless "Y'' phase to a gapped "up-up-down'' phase is obtained, as predicted by spin-wave theory. Most importantly, around $J_2/J_1=1/8$, a condensate of monopoles (which are gapless gauge excitations of the spin liquid at $H=0$) is stabilized in a significant region of the phase diagram, for small Zeeman fields. Here, a finite scalar chirality is present, while no transverse magnetic order is detected. The stability of the monopole phase is confirmed by a field-theory approach that includes a self-consistent random-phase approximation of the low-lying spin fluctuations. The boundary between the monopole and "Y'' phases is also obtained with no free parameters.

</details>


### [123] [Monopole Spin Density Wave States in Magnetic Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14829)
*Xi Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在Weyl半金属中引入了单极自旋密度波（SDW）态，该态源于同手性Weyl节点之间嵌套费米面的粒子-空穴配对。SDW序参量具有非平凡的配对Berry相位，由单极调和函数描述，并在能隙函数中呈现拓扑保护的节点结构。通过晶格模型，展示了螺旋和摆线SDW序在能带、费米弧和表面自旋极化中的不同特征，且量子几何张量在弱耦合极限下实现了理想量子几何。研究统一了不同配对通道的单极有序态理解，并为ReAlX材料中磁性有序的区分及拓扑自旋电子学应用提供了途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Weyl半金属中，拓扑与磁性的相互作用是新型量子相的重要来源。尽管超导和电荷密度波中的单极调和序参量已被建立，自旋密度波对应的单极序尚属空白。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索同手性Weyl节点间粒子-空穴配对产生的单极SDW态及其拓扑性质。

Method: 理论推导单极SDW序参量的配对Berry相位和单极调和函数描述；构建晶格模型分别研究螺旋和摆线SDW序；通过能带结构、费米弧分布和表面自旋极化模式分析其特征；计算量子几何张量以揭示理想量子几何的实现条件。

Result: 单极SDW序参量具有非平凡的配对Berry相位，由单极调和函数描述且能隙函数中存在拓扑保护节点；螺旋和摆线SDW在能带、费米弧和表面自旋极化上产生可区分的特征，可通过自旋和角分辨光电子能谱直接观测；弱耦合极限下量子几何张量实现理想量子几何，量子距离涨落完全由Berry曲率主导。

Conclusion: 本文统一了不同配对通道（超导、电荷密度波、自旋密度波）中单极有序态的理论框架；为ReAlX材料中竞争磁性有序的区分提供了实验探测方案；揭示了单极配对体系中理想量子几何的实现机制，为拓扑自旋电子学应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: The interplay between topology and magnetism in Weyl semimetals has recently emerged as a fertile ground for novel quantum phases. While monopole harmonic order parameters have been established for superconductivity and charge density waves in these systems, their spin density wave counterparts remain unexplored. Here we introduce monopole spin density wave (SDW) states arising from particle-hole pairing between nested Fermi surfaces enclosing Weyl nodes of the same chirality. We demonstrate that the SDW order parameter inherits a nontrivial pairing Berry phase and is described by monopole harmonic functions that exhibit topologically protected nodal structures in the gap function. Through a concrete lattice model, we show that helical and cycloidal SDW orders produce distinct signatures in band structures, Fermi arc distributions, and surface spin polarization patterns, which can be directly resolved by spin- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy. Remarkably, we find that the quantum geometric tensor of the monopole pairing realizes ideal quantum geometry in the weak-coupling limit, where quantum distance fluctuations are entirely governed by Berry curvature. Our results not only unify the understanding of monopole ordered states across pairing channels but also provide experimental avenues for distinguishing competing magnetic orders in ReAlX (Re=rare earth elements, X=Si, Ge) materials and suggest potential applications in topological spintronics.

</details>


### [124] [Optimization dynamics of Transformer backflow neural quantum states for the two-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14875)
*Zong-Yu Liao,Jia-Qi Wang,Rong-Qiang He,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Transformer反流神经量子态（NQS）在多阶段训练工作流中的优化动力学，重点分析了更新阈值、Transformer宽度、行列式通道数和蒙特卡洛批次大小四个超参数对掺杂二维Hubbard模型收敛性能的影响，并验证了工作流在不同条件下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Transformer反流NQS的优化动力学如何依赖于关键优化和架构超参数，以便理解表达性、更新稳定性与采样质量之间的权衡。

Method: 基于多行列式Transformer反流NQS架构，采用三阶段训练流程（NNB初始化、监督Transformer预训练、MARCH主能量优化），在掺杂4×4周期性Hubbard模型（U=8）上系统扫描更新阈值、Transformer宽度、行列式通道数和批次大小的影响，并在半填充、弱相互作用、开放边界及8×8晶格上测试泛化性。

Result: 适度更新约束可提升MARCH优化效率；更宽的Transformer和更多行列式通道增强表达容量；更大的批次降低采样噪声。

Conclusion: 识别了Transformer反流NQS的实用优化趋势，强调了表达性、更新稳定性与采样质量之间的平衡。

Abstract: Building on the multi-determinant Transformer backflow neural quantum state (NQS) ansatz and the associated multi-stage training workflow for the doped two-dimensional Hubbard model, we investigate how the optimization dynamics of the NQS depend on several key optimization and architectural hyperparameters. The workflow consists of neural-network backflow (NNB) initialization, supervised Transformer pre-training, and main energy optimization using the Moment-Adaptive ReConfiguration Heuristic (MARCH) within variational Monte Carlo. Using the doped $4\times4$ periodic Hubbard model at $U=8$ as a baseline, we examine how the update-norm threshold, Transformer width, number of determinant channels, and Monte Carlo batch size affect convergence. We find that a moderate update constraint improves the efficiency of MARCH optimization, larger Transformer width and more determinant channels improve the expressive capacity of the ansatz, and larger Monte Carlo batches reduce sampling noise in the update direction. We further test the same workflow at half filling, weaker interaction strength, open boundary conditions, and on a larger $8\times8$ doped lattice. These results identify practical optimization trends for Transformer backflow NQSs and highlight the balance between ansatz expressivity, MARCH update stability, and Monte Carlo sampling quality.

</details>


### [125] [Sizable Ligand-Mediated Bond-Dependent Interactions in a Spin-1 Triangular Antiferromagnet NiI$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14893)
*Hao Xu,Weiqin Zhu,Shufan Cheng,Yanyan Shangguan,Song Bao,Junbo Liao,Bo Zhang,Zihang Song,Shuai Dong,Maofeng Wu,Stanislav E. Nikitin,Travis J. Williams,Changsong Xu,Jinsheng Wen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过综合多种实验和理论手段，在NiI₂中发现了由配体驱动的强Kitaev相互作用，为在弱自旋-轨道耦合磁性离子体系中实现Kitaev模型提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kitaev相互作用是量子自旋液体和分数激发的基础，但实验实现非常稀缺，亟需寻找新的材料体系和机制。

Method: 结合非弹性中子散射、磁化测量、磁结构分析、第一性原理计算和线性自旋波模拟等多种手段。

Result: 确定了包含显著Kitaev相互作用和离对角Γ相互作用的最小模型，这些作用稳定了倾斜磁基态并在自旋波谱中打开了能隙。

Conclusion: 强自旋-轨道耦合配体离子可在磁性离子本身弱自旋-轨道耦合的体系中产生强键依赖各向异性，为扩展Kitaev材料范围提供了具体路径。

Abstract: The bond-dependent anisotropic Kitaev interactions are the key for the Kitaev model, which has attracted intense interest for its potential to host quantum-spin-liquid states and fractional excitations. However, experimental realizations of such interactions remain scarce. Here, we investigate the magnetic excitations of NiI$_2$, a van der Waals magnet with spin $S=1$. By combining inelastic neutron scattering, magnetization measurements, magnetic structure analysis, first-principles calculations, and linear-spin-wave simulations, we identify a minimal model that features substantial Kitaev and off-diagonal $Γ$ interactions, which together stabilize the canted magnetic ground state and open a gap in the spin-wave spectrum. Notably, these interactions arise from strong spin-orbit coupling on the ligand ions, despite the quenched orbital moment of the magnetic Ni$^{2+}$ ions. Our results provide compelling experimental evidence for the ligand-driven Kitaev mechanism. This demonstrates a concrete pathway to generating strong bond-dependent anisotropy in systems where the magnetic ions themselves have weak spin-orbit coupling, thereby substantially broadening the range of potential Kitaev materials.

</details>


### [126] [Derivation and application of a general scaling relation between the dc and asymptotic high-frequency optical Hall responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15043)
*E. Abelev,R. Romero,N. P. Armitage*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于Kramers-Krong关系，推导并应用了一个将低频准直流霍尔电导率与高频光学霍尔响应（及其在Kerr和Faraday效应中的表现）联系起来的通用表达式，适用于时间反演对称性破缺体系如铁磁体、超导体和磁场中的金属。通过参数化图验证了该关系在单模回旋共振情况下的精确性，并在铁磁体和TRSB超导体的理论模型中近似成立。将该关系应用于多种铁磁系统的实验数据，结果合理。利用该关系预测铜氧化物超导体在赝隙温度下应表现出约0.1 Ω⁻¹·cm⁻¹的反常霍尔效应，该值虽小但在实验可及范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立低频准直流霍尔电导率与高频光学霍尔响应之间的普适关系，从而能够通过更容易测量的光学Kerr/Faraday效应来推断难以直接测量的直流霍尔电导率，特别适用于时间反演对称性破缺体系。

Method: 基于Kramers-Krong色散关系推导出连接低频与高频霍尔响应的通用解析表达式，通过参数化图验证单模回旋共振情况下的精确性，并在铁磁体和TRSB超导体的理论模型中进行近似验证，最后应用于多种铁磁系统的实验数据。

Result: 该关系在单模回旋共振情况下精确成立，在铁磁体和TRSB超导体的理论模型中近似成立，在多种铁磁系统的实验数据中也表现出合理的一致性。预测铜氧化物超导体在赝隙温度下存在约0.1 Ω⁻¹·cm⁻¹的反常霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 推导的Kramers-Krong关系是连接低频准直流霍尔电导率与高频光学霍尔响应的有效工具，可应用于多种时间反演对称性破缺体系。对铜氧化物超导体的反常霍尔效应预测虽值小但实验可及，值得实验验证。该工作与基于量子几何关系设定物理量边界的努力具有一定相似性。

Abstract: Based on the Kramers-Krong relations, we derive and apply a quite general expression that that relates the low frequency quasi-dc Hall conductivity to the asymptotic high frequency optical Hall response (and its manifestations in Kerr and Faraday effects) for time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB) states of matter like ferromagnets and time-reversal symmetry breaking superconductors as well as metals in magnetic field. Parametric plots shows this relation is obeyed exactly for the trivial single-mode case of sharp cyclotron resonance, and approximately for theoretical models for ferromagnets and TRSB superconductors. We also apply it to the experimental data from a variety of ferromagnetic systems and show reasonable agreement there as well. We use the relation to predict, from the size of the spontaneous Kerr effect at the pseudogap temperature of the cuprate superconductors that cuprates should exhibit an anomalous Hall effect of approximately 0.1 Ohm$^{-1}\cdot$cm$^{-1}$. This is a small value, but one within experimental reach and we encourage the search for it. Although not explicitly quantum geometric, our treatment has some similarities to efforts to set bounds on physical quantities based on quantum geometric relations and limited physical information.

</details>


### [127] [Fast two-dimensional tensor-network contraction via subspace iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15158)
*Yining Zhang,Philippe Corboz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于子空间迭代的CTMRG方法（SI-CTMRG），通过QR分解替代大矩阵SVD，将主要计算开销从矩阵分解转移到张量收缩，从而在GPU上实现高达两个数量级的加速，并在单个H100 GPU上约10小时内获得了三角晶格海森堡反铁磁体的最新iPEPS结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CTMRG的计算成本主要来自重复的截断奇异值分解（SVD），这限制了其在GPU上的加速效率。

Method: 引入子空间迭代CTMRG（SI-CTMRG），采用基于QR分解的投影子构造方法，将大规模矩阵SVD替换为小规模矩阵的SVD，从而将主要计算代价从分解操作转移到张量收缩操作。

Result: 相比标准CTMRG实现高达两个数量级的加速；在单个H100 GPU上约10小时即可达到三角晶格海森堡反铁磁体的最先进iPEPS结果。

Conclusion: SI-CTMRG方法高度适合GPU加速，显著提升了CTMRG的计算效率，能够以更低的计算成本达到先进水平的结果。

Abstract: The corner transfer matrix renormalization group (CTMRG) is one of the standard contraction methods for infinite projected entangled-pair states (iPEPS), but its computational cost is dominated by repeated truncated singular value decompositions (SVDs). We introduce subspace-iteration CTMRG (SI-CTMRG), a QR-based projector construction that replaces each large-matrix SVD with an SVD of a much smaller matrix. The resulting algorithm shifts the dominant cost from decompositions to tensor contractions, making it highly suited to GPU acceleration and yielding speedups of up to two orders of magnitude over standard CTMRG. We demonstrate the efficiency and accuracy of the method for the triangular-lattice Heisenberg antiferromagnet, reaching state-of-the-art iPEPS results on a single H100 GPU in approximately 10 hours of computation.

</details>


### [128] [Driven-dissipative superconductivity in moiré heterostructure without attraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15169)
*Tsung-Sheng Huang,Atac Imamoglu,Mohammad Hafezi,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过驱动-耗散协议在二维莫尔异质结构中制备超导态的方法，利用层自由度作为赝自旋，通过光学诱导空间操作实现配对，并展示了局域耗散与集体耗散下不同的物理行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超导制备依赖增强吸引相互作用，本文旨在探索一种不依赖于增强吸引相互作用的替代路径，即通过耗散方法制备量子有序态（超导态）。

Method: 采用驱动-耗散协议，利用双层莫尔平台中层自由度作为赝自旋，通过光学诱导空间操作实现配对所需的赝自旋结构；局域耗散由异质结构中弱色散的玻色子模式自然提供。

Result: 在局域耗散下可实现稳态超导；在集体耗散下，同一平台在早期表现出超辐射爆发。

Conclusion: 驱动-耗散莫尔异质结构是制备超导态的有前景平台，并揭示了稳态配对与瞬态超辐射之间的内在联系。

Abstract: Dissipative preparation of quantum order offers a route to superconductivity that does not rely on enhancing attractive interactions. Here we propose a driven-dissipative protocol to prepare superconductivity as a stationary state of a two-dimensional moiré heterostructure. The key ingredient is a bilayer moiré platform in which the layer degree of freedom acts as a pseudospin, allowing the pseudospin structure required for pairing to be implemented through optically induced spatial operations. This preparation scheme requires local dissipation, which we show to arises naturally from weakly dispersive bosonic modes in the heterostructure. In contrast, in the opposite regime of collective dissipation, the same platform exhibits an early-time superradiant burst. Our results establish driven-dissipative moiré heterostructures as a promising platform for preparing superconductivity, while also revealing a connection between steady-state pairing and transient superradiance.

</details>


### [129] [Fermiology of the kagome compound LuNb6Sn6 probed by de Haas-van Alphen oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15248)
*Tucker Beekmann,Caue Kaufmann Ribeiro,Kyryl Shtefiienko,Jiaqiang Yan,Brenden R. Ortiz,Christopher A. Mizzi,Keshav Shrestha*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过磁扭矩、磁化和热容测量，对Kagome金属LuNb6Sn6进行了详细的de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA)研究。实验发现85 K处存在一阶电荷密度波（CDW）相变，观测到两个主要dHvA频率（F_alpha ~ 20 T和F_beta ~ 200 T），其中F_alpha口袋具有非平庸Berry相位，表明可能存在非平庸电子拓扑。第一性原理计算揭示了狄拉克态、平带和多个范霍夫奇点，但基于原始相的计算无法完全复现实验观测的量子振荡频率，表明CDW诱导的费米面重构在基态电子结构中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome材料因其独特的几何阻挫电子结构而备受关注，但对其费米面拓扑和量子输运性质的理解尚不充分。本文旨在通过高精度的dHvA实验结合第一性原理计算，深入揭示LuNb6Sn6的电子结构和费米面拓扑特征，特别是CDW相变对电子结构的影响。

Method: 采用磁扭矩测量、磁化测量和热容测量进行实验研究；利用Landau扇图分析和Lifshitz-Kosevich公式分析量子振荡的温度和磁场依赖性；结合第一性原理计算进行电子结构分析。

Result: 发现T_CDW = 85 K处的一阶CDW相变；识别出F_alpha ~ 20 T和F_beta ~ 200 T两个主要dHvA频率，其角依赖性符合椭球费米面；F_alpha口袋存在非平庸Berry相位；提取的各向异性电子参数揭示了量子输运的各向异性；第一性原理计算预测了狄拉克带交叉、平带和多个范霍夫奇点，但与实验频率不完全一致。

Conclusion: LuNb6Sn6中存在由CDW诱导的费米面重构，这对其基态电子结构至关重要。这些发现丰富了我们对Kagome材料费米面拓扑和电子结构的理解，为后续研究提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We report a detailed de Haas-van Alphen (dHvA) study of the recently discovered kagome metal LuNb6Sn6 using torque magnetometry, magnetization, and heat-capacity measurements. Temperature-dependent torque and heat-capacity data reveal a charge density wave (CDW) transition at T_CDW = 85 K. The thermal hysteresis observed in both measurements establishes the first-order nature of the transition. Quantum oscillation measurements identify two major dHvA frequencies: F_alpha ~ 20 T and F_beta ~ 200 T, and their angular dependence is consistent with ellipsoidal Fermi surface (FS) pockets. Landau fan diagram analysis reveals evidence for a nontrivial Berry phase associated with the F_alpha pocket, indicating possible nontrivial electronic topology in LuNb6Sn6. Analysis of the temperature and magnetic field dependence of the oscillations using the Lifshitz-Kosevich formula yields electronic parameters that indicate anisotropic quantum transport properties. First-principles calculations provide further insight into the electronic structure, revealing Dirac-like band crossings, a flat band, and multiple van Hove singularities near the Fermi level. Our calculations based on the pristine phase cannot fully reproduce the experimentally observed quantum oscillation frequencies, suggesting that CDW-induced FS reconstruction plays a crucial role in the ground-state electronic structure of LuNb6Sn6. These results provide new insight into the FS topology and electronic structure of LuNb6Sn6, enriching our understanding of the electronic properties of kagome materials.

</details>


### [130] [Single-component twisted $\mathbb{Z}_3$ orthogonal metal in an $e/3$-anyon fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15274)
*Zhaoyu Han,Ashvin Vishwanath,Eslam Khalaf*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出一种掺杂1/3分数量子陈绝缘体上出现的新型金属相——扭曲Z3正交金属，表现为电子准粒子权重消失但存在电荷e/3费米子的费米面，配对后可产生任意手征中心荷的任意子超导态，并具有独特的分数费米学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索掺杂分数量子陈绝缘体后出现的非常规金属相，并以此作为母态构建具有任意手征中心荷的任意子超导态，超越基于2e/3任意子的传统任意子超导构造方案。

Method: 提出扭曲Z3正交金属相的理论框架：电荷e/3费米子与Dijkgraaf-Witten扭曲Z3规范场耦合；构建显式波函数ansatz；分析任意子间谷间排斥对相的稳定作用；论证该构造适用于高阶Laughlin态但不适用于Jain态。

Result: 发现配对该分数化费米面可消除拓扑序并产生一系列超导体，其手征中心荷由配对e/3费米子的BdG能带拓扑直接决定，物理序参量的角动量被约束为3的倍数；金属相本身具有e/3散粒噪声、反常量子振荡和6π交流约瑟夫森效应等分数费米学特征。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一条通向超越传统任意子超导构造方案的超导态新路径，并预言了可在实验中探测的独特输运信号。

Abstract: We propose an unconventional metallic phase emerging on doping the $1/3$ Fractional Chern insulator, which can serve as a parent state to anyon superconductivity with arbitrary chiral central charge. It is a twisted $\mathbb{Z}_3$ orthogonal metal: a state with vanishing electron quasiparticle weight but a single well-defined Fermi surface of emergent charge-$e/3$ fermions coupled to a Dijkgraaf-Witten twisted $\mathbb{Z}_3$ gauge field. It is manifestly valley-symmetric and valley-gapped. Pairing this fractionalized Fermi surface then removes the topological order and produces a family of superconductors whose chiral central charge is directly set by the BdG band topology of the paired $e/3$ fermions, while the angular momentum of the physical order parameter is constrained to be a multiple of three. This scenario provides a route to superconducting states beyond anyon-superconductivity constructions based on $2e/3$ anyons. The metallic phase itself carries distinctive ``fractional Fermiology'' signatures: $e/3$ shot noise, anomalous quantum oscillations, and a $6π$ ac Josephson effect when it mediates the Josephson coupling between superconductors. We construct explicit wavefunction ansatz realizing the phase, and argue that inter-valley repulsion between anyons stabilizes this phase compared to competing states. We show that the construction extends to higher Laughlin states but not to Jain states.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Beyond the Riemann Hypothesis bounds: A pair-correlation approach to the least prime in arithmetic progression and the smallest quadratic non-residue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14515)
*Neelam Kandhil,Alessandro Languasco,Pieter Moree*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明，在广义黎曼假设（GRH）下，结合非平凡零点的垂直间距（对关联）信息，可以超越经典的最小平素数及最小二次非剩余的上界估计，建立了更优的定量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果仅依赖GRH（零点水平位置）来估计算术数列中的最小素数和最小二次非剩余，但无法进一步突破。本文试图引入零点的垂直间距（对关联）信息来改进这些经典上界。

Method: 将GRH（零点水平分布）与Dirichlet L-函数的对关联假设（零点垂直分布）相结合，利用两种零点分布视角，在解析数论框架下推导新的上界估计。

Result: 在GRH加特定对关联假设下，为最小素数和最小二次非剩余问题建立了比经典GRH结果更优的上界估计。

Conclusion: 零点的垂直间距（对关联）信息可有效改进最小素数及最小二次非剩余的已知上界，建立了对关联现象与这两个经典数论问题之间的新联系。

Abstract: The Generalized Riemann Hypothesis (GRH) has long defined the expected bounds for the smallest prime in an arithmetic progression and the least quadratic non-residue. However, this hypothesis primarily addresses the horizontal location of non-trivial zeros. In this paper, we show that incorporating the vertical spacing--or pair-correlation--of these zeros allows us to surpass these classical bounds. By combining these two zero-distribution perspectives, we establish sharper estimates for both problems under GRH and specific pair-correlation hypotheses, thereby providing a new link between pair-correlation phenomena for Dirichlet L-functions and these two classical problems.

</details>


### [132] [The Spine: A Supersingular Highway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14572)
*Taha Hedayat,Renate Scheidler,Sarah Arpin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了超奇异ℓ-同源图脊柱在ℓ=2且p≡71,119 (mod 120)情况下的结构，给出了移除非平凡边后各分量的距离、离心率和直径函数，并利用平均直径区分不同脊柱结构，为脊柱结构随p变化的规律提供了启发式分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arXiv:2502.03613未能完整描述超奇异ℓ-同源图脊柱在ℓ=2且p≡71,119 (mod 120)情况下的结构，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过计算脊柱各分量在移除非平凡边（未定义于𝔽_p上）后的距离、离心率和直径函数，并利用平均直径来区分不同结构。

Result: 得到了各分量的距离、离心率和直径函数，并利用平均直径成功区分了不同的脊柱结构。

Conclusion: 通过显式计算为脊柱结构随p变化的行为提供了启发性分析，表明平均直径可作为区分不同结构的有效指标。

Abstract: We consider the structure of the spine of the supersingular $\ell$-isogeny graph for one of the cases which arXiv:2502.03613 was not able to fully describe, $\ell = 2$ and $p = 71, 119\pmod{120}$. We find the distance, eccentricity, and diameter functions, of the components of the spine without the non-trivial edge not defined over $\mathbb{F}_p$. Using these functions, we find the mean diameter of the spine and show how this value distinguishes the different structures of the spine. Thus, allowing us to use explicit computations to provide heuristics on the behavior of the spine's structure as $p$ varies.

</details>


### [133] [The First Moment of Dirichlet L-functions over Generators of the Character Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14612)
*Rizwanur Khan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了当q通过素数趋于无穷时，模q的狄利克雷特征群生成元上中心值L(1/2,χ)的平均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究狄利克雷L函数在特殊点1/2处的中心值在特征群生成元上的平均行为，这是解析数论中L函数值分布的重要问题。

Method: 通过对模q的狄利克雷特征群生成元上的L(1/2,χ)进行平均，并令q通过素数趋于无穷。

Result: 得到了当q通过素数趋于无穷时，狄利克雷特征群生成元上中心值L(1/2,χ)的平均值。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了狄利克雷L函数在临界线上中心值的平均分布性质，为理解L函数值的统计行为提供了新的见解。

Abstract: We evaluate the average of the central values $L(1/2,χ)$ over the generators of the group of Dirichlet characters modulo $q$, as $q\to\infty$ through the primes.

</details>


### [134] [Minimal degree of an isogeny between a supersingular elliptic curve and its conjugate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14624)
*Yves Aubry,Roger Oyono,Christelle Vincent*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了超奇异椭圆曲线 $E$ 与其共轭曲线 $E^{(p)}$ 之间的同源最小次数，给出了依赖于 $p$ 的界，证明该界在渐近意义上是最优的且在多数情况下是紧的，并通过计算同源格的成功最小值提出了若干猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超奇异椭圆曲线及其共轭之间的同源是数论与密码学中的重要研究对象，但其最小次数缺乏系统刻画。本文旨在给出一个依赖于 $p$ 的最优界，并通过计算实验验证其性质。

Method: 发展了一种计算从超奇异椭圆曲线到其共轭的特定同源次数的新技术，通过对包含这些同源的格进行成功最小值（successive minima）的广泛计算来得到界。

Result: 给出了从 $E$ 到 $E^{(p)}$ 的同源最小次数的界，证明了该界渐近最优且在多数情况下是紧的；同时提供了大量格成功最小值的计算结果。

Conclusion: 该界是最优的且在许多情况下是紧的；基于计算数据提出了若干猜想，包括关于使该界被取到的素数 $p$ 的集合的猜想。

Abstract: Let $E$ be a supersingular elliptic curve defined over $\bar{\mathbb{F}}_p$ and $E^{(p)}$ be its conjugate. We give a bound on the minimal degree of an isogeny from $E$ to $E^{(p)}$ depending on $p$, and show that this bound is both asymptotically optimal as well as sharp in many cases. This bound is obtained by developing a new technique to compute the degree of certain isogenies from a supersingular elliptic curve to its conjugate, and we present extensive computations of the successive minima of the lattice containing these isogenies. Following this, we give several conjectures supported by the data we have obtained, including some on the set of primes $p$ for which the bound we give in this article is attained.

</details>


### [135] [Multiple Clausen values and deformed Apéry-like series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14646)
*Zhi-Wei Sun,Yajun Zhou*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过推广的中心二项式系数，将变形Apéry级数表示为多重Clausen值（MCVs），并利用MCVs的代数关系推导出两个具体的闭式表达式，涉及L-函数和多重zeta值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义中心二项式系数倒数相关级数的解析表示，建立其与多重Clausen值（MCVs）这一特殊CMZVs类的联系，从而获得闭式表达式。

Method: 通过欧拉Gamma函数定义广义中心二项式系数，构造变形Apéry级数$\mathscr A_{s,n}$，利用MCVs（3级分圆多重zeta值）的代数关系进行化简和闭式推导。

Result: 给出了两个具体例子：$\mathscr A_{1,5}$用Dirichlet L-函数$L(\chi_{-3},6), L(\chi_{-3},4), L(\chi_{-3},2)$表示；$\mathscr A_{4,4}$用多重zeta值$\zeta_{5,3}$和$\pi^8$的有理倍数表示。

Conclusion: 证明了变形Apéry级数可以通过MCVs的代数关系得到闭式表达，为相关级数的解析求值提供了新途径。

Abstract: With generalized central binomial coefficients $ \binom{2x}{x}:=\frac{Γ(2x+1)}{[Γ(x+1)]^2}$ defined through Euler's gamma function, we represent deformed Apéry-like series \[ \mathscr A_{s,n}:=\sum_{k=1}^\infty\left.\!\frac{\partial^n}{\partial x^n}\frac{1}{x^s\binom{2x}{x}}\right|_{x=k} \] by multiple Clausen values (MCVs), which belong to a special class of cyclotomic multiple zeta values (CMZVs) at level $3$. For example, exploiting provable algebraic relations among MCVs, we show that \[\mathscr A_{1,5}=-\frac{9[495L(χ_{-3},6)-30π^{2}L(χ_{-3},4)-2π^{4}L(χ_{-3},2)]}{4}\]and\[\mathscr A_{4,4}=\frac{352ζ_{5,3}}{15}+\frac{752537π^{8}}{10206000},\]where $ L(χ_{-3},s):=\sum_{n=0}^\infty\left[(3n+1)^{-s}-(3n+2)^{-s}\right]$ and $ ζ_{5,3}:=\sum_{m=1}^\infty\sum_{n=1}^{m-1}m^{-5}n^{-3}$.

</details>


### [136] [Equidistribution and the torsor method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14866)
*Christian Bernert,Ulrich Derenthal,Florian Wilsch*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了数域上光滑分裂五次 del Pezzo 曲面在反典范高度下直线外有理点的等分布性和 Manin 猜想，其证明基于一个通用定理，该定理可通过使用多个等价高度函数的 torsor 方法处理 Manin 问题的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究数域上光滑分裂五次 del Pezzo 曲面上直线外有理点的分布规律，验证 Manin 猜想在该情形下的成立性。

Method: 基于一个通用定理，利用 torsor 方法结合多个等价高度函数来处理 Manin 问题的变体，进而完成证明。

Result: 证明了在反典范高度下，数域上光滑分裂五次 del Pezzo 曲面上直线外有理点满足等分布性且 Manin 猜想成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用 torsor 方法处理 Manin 猜想相关变体提供了可广泛应用的通用框架，并具体验证了五次 del Pezzo 曲面上的情形。

Abstract: We prove equidistribution and Manin's conjecture for rational points outside the lines on smooth split quintic del Pezzo surfaces over number fields with respect to any anticanonical height. The proof is based on a general theorem that is broadly usable to deduce equidistribution when using the torsor method with several equivalent height functions to treat variants of Manin's problem.

</details>


### [137] [On the Log-Concavity of the D'Arcais Polynomials for Normalised Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14961)
*Johann Stumpenhusen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了非负实数序列的对数凹性，并将其推广到多项式族，提出了一种新变体，证明了某些类型的D'Arcais多项式在特定点具有该性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数凹性在组合数学、概率论和数论中有重要应用。已有的对多项式族对数凹性的推广尚不完善，需要提出新变体以覆盖更多情况，特别是D'Arcais多项式这类特殊函数。

Method: 引入了一种新的对数凹性变体定义，然后针对特定类型的D'Arcais多项式，在特定点处验证该性质是否成立。

Result: 证明了某些类型的D'Arcais多项式在所提出的新对数凹性变体下，在特定点处满足该性质。

Conclusion: 提出的新变体成功扩展了对数凹性的适用范围，并为D'Arcais多项式的结构性质提供了新的见解。

Abstract: A sequence $(a_n)_{n \in \mathbb{N}}$ of non-negative real numbers is called log-concave at $n$ if $a_n^2 \geq a_{n+1}a_{n-1}$. This property has been generalised in various ways to families of polynomials. We introduce a new variant and show that certain types of D'Arcais polynomials have the respective properties at certain points.

</details>


### [138] [Double $L$-values of Negative Weight Admitting Series Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15037)
*Daiki Iju,Takashi Nakamura*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Thue-Morse序列构造了具有级数表示的负权双重L-值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有级数表示形式的负权双重L-值，扩展L-值理论的研究范围。

Method: 使用组合数学中的Thue-Morse序列（一种自动序列）作为工具进行构造。

Result: 成功构造了负权双重L-值，并赋予其级数表示形式。

Conclusion: Thue-Morse序列可有效用于负权双重L-值的构造，为该领域提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct double $L$-values of negative weight that admit series representations. In our construction, we use the Thue-Morse sequence, which appears in combinatorics on words and automatic sequences.

</details>


### [139] [On a character-twisted analogue of Schäffer's equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15090)
*Kálmán Györy,Vandita Patel,Ákos Pintér,Samir Siksek*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于由正整数f定义的原始二次特征χ，当对应的k阶伯努利多项式B_k(χ,X)不可约且次数至少为2时，对于100%的能被f整除的正整数m，涉及特征和与幂和的丢番图方程在整数范围内无解（n≥2）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类由原始二次特征定义的幂和丢番图方程的可解性问题，探索在何种条件下该类方程几乎必然无整数解。

Method: 利用伯努利多项式B_k(χ,X)的不可约性和次数条件，结合数论工具证明对于几乎所有满足整除条件的正整数m，该丢番图方程无整数解。

Result: 当B_k(χ,X)不可约且次数≥2时，对于100%的满足f|m的正整数m，方程无整数解（x,y,n∈Z, n≥2）。

Conclusion: 该丢番图方程在几乎所有满足条件的情况下均无解，结果具有渐近完备性（100%的m）。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a positive integer, and let $χ$ be a primitive quadratic character of conductor $f$. Let $k$ be a positive integer, and write $B_k(χ,X)$ for the $k$-th Bernoulli polynomial corresponding to $χ$. Suppose $B_k(χ,X)$ is irreducible and of degree at least $2$. Then for 100% of positive integers $m$ divisible by $f$, the Diophantine equation \[
  χ(1) \cdot (x+1)^k+χ(2) \cdot (x+2)^k+\cdots+χ(m) \cdot (x+m)^k \, =\, y^n, \] has no solutions with $x$, $y$, $n$ integers, and $n \ge 2$.

</details>


### [140] [A Bombieri-Vinogradov theorem for exponential sums over products of k primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15137)
*Pierre-Alexandre Bazin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于k个素数乘积上的指数和的Bombieri-Vinogradov型定理，并应用该定理得到了一个下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有固定素数因子个数（Ω(m)=k）的自然数上的指数和的上确界下界问题，这是解析数论中关于指数和均匀分布性质的重要问题。

Method: 通过建立Bombieri-Vinogradov型定理来处理k个素数乘积上的指数和，然后利用该定理推导出下界估计。

Result: 对于2≤k≤(2-ε)log log x和α∈ℝ，得到下界 sup_{n≤x} |∑_{m≤n} 1_{Ω(m)=k} e(αm)| ≫ x^{1/6-ε}。

Conclusion: 所证明的Bombieri-Vinogradov型定理是有效的工具，能够导出k-殆素数上指数和的非平凡下界。

Abstract: We prove a Bombieri-Vinogradov type theorem for exponential sums over products of $k$ primes. As an application, we show the lower bound $$\sup_{n\le x} \left|\sum_{m\le n} 1_{Ω(m) = k} e(αm)\right| \gg x^{1/6 - \varepsilon}$$ for $2\le k\le (2-\varepsilon)\log\log x$ and $α\in\mathbb{R},$ where we noted $e(β) := e^{2iπβ}.$

</details>
